Você é o revisor independente (Codex) no protocolo Claude↔Codex deste projeto (expiration-tracker, ver AGENTS.md §4). Avalie a proposta de design abaixo (Rodada 1) para fechar o achado P0.2 da auditoria externa 2026-09-11: GuestSession não está vinculada ao credential/token do path, permitindo que duas abas com dois links de guest diferentes colidam (a sessão da segunda aba sobrescreve o cookie global da primeira, e mutações da primeira aba passam a resolver contra o DocumentRequest errado).

Leia o arquivo completo em docs/architecture/reviews/guest-session-binding-scoping/round1-claude-proposal.md (relativo à raiz do repo) antes de avaliar — ele contém contexto verificado no código, pesquisa externa (OWASP IDOR/Session Management), checklist de critérios derivado da pesquisa, e o design proposto em detalhe.

Verifique especificamente, lendo o código real do repositório (não confie cegamente na descrição da proposta):
1. src/modules/document-archive/domain/guest-session.ts (campos documentRequestId/credentialSelectorHash, confirmar que já existem e o que documentam hoje)
2. src/modules/document-archive/application/guest-document-access-service.ts (submitEvidence()/confirmUploadInFlight(), resolveSession(), parseRequestAccessToken, hmacRequestAccessCrypto.hash — confirmar que o parser e o HMAC já existem e podem ser reaproveitados como a proposta diz)
3. src/modules/document-archive/http/document-archive-guest-handlers.ts (handleSubmitEvidence linhas ~192-201 e handleConfirmUpload — confirmar o comentário existente sobre requireToken ser "for route symmetry/observability" e nunca usado para autorização)
4. src/modules/document-archive/domain/request-access-credential.ts (confirmar que RequestAccessCredential e GuestSession usam o mesmo esquema selector.secret)
5. Qualquer outra rota guest que resolva só por path token (handleGetGuestRequest/handleStartGuestSession/handleListGuestDocumentTypes) — confirmar se a proposta está certa que elas não precisam de mudança

Dê uma nota de 0 a 10 (não arredondada) avaliando:
- Correção técnica: o vínculo por credentialSelectorHash realmente fecha o cenário de 2 abas sem quebrar nenhum fluxo legítimo (guest com uma única aba, sessão renovada, etc.)?
- Segurança: a comparação sem timingSafeEqual é de fato segura (comparação de identificador não-secreto, não de segredo)? O colapso no mesmo erro genérico realmente evita uma nova superfície de enumeração/timing?
- Completude: falta algum caso real (ex: sessão expirada durante o vínculo, path token de formato inválido, DocumentRequest cancelado entre a emissão da sessão e a mutação)?
- Escopo: a proposta reabre alguma decisão já aprovada sem necessidade (ex: reautenticar o secret do credential), ou expande escopo além do gap nomeado (as 2 rotas afetadas)?
- Achados REAIS de bugs/gaps na proposta (não estilo, não preferência).

Formato da resposta:
NOTA: X,X/10
VEREDITO: APPROVED (se >=9,0) ou NEEDS FIXES (se <9,0)
ACHADOS: lista numerada, cada um com severidade (BLOQUEANTE/MENOR) e localização específica na proposta ou no código

Este diretÃ³rio trata de **como o trabalho de engenharia Ã© medido e revisado** (rubrica, critÃ©rios por eixo, protocolo de debate, achados de auditoria) â€” nÃ£o confundir com `docs/architecture/`, que trata do que o sistema Ã‰ (design, modelo de dados, decisÃµes de arquitetura). ## PrecedÃªncia de fontes (quando houver divergÃªncia)

Mesma regra de `docs/architecture/README.md` â€” `AGENTS.md` (raiz) sempre vence sobre conteÃºdo deste diretÃ³rio; um achado de auditoria (`reviews/full-audit-*-summary.md`) Ã© evidÃªncia de uma rodada especÃ­fica, nunca redefine os pesos/critÃ©rios de `joint-review-criteria.md` (ele mesmo linka para lÃ¡ em vez de duplicar, ver seu prÃ³prio rodapÃ© "Como adicionar um novo eixo").

## `reviews/` â€” convenÃ§Ã£o de nomenclatura e classificaÃ§Ã£o

EvidÃªncia de rodada, mesmo papel que `docs/architecture/history/` tem para decisÃµes de arquitetura: nunca normativo, sÃ³ prova de como uma nota/decisÃ£o foi alcanÃ§ada. TrÃªs geraÃ§Ãµes de conteÃºdo coexistem aqui, nesta ordem cronolÃ³gica:

1. **Checkpoints da Engineering Maturity Review** (`checkpoint-01-rubric/`, `checkpoint-02-09-consolidated/`, `checkpoint-12-redteam/`, e os arquivos soltos `_codex-*-checkpoint1-*.txt` no nÃ­vel raiz de `reviews/`) â€” produziram a rubrica CONGELADA (`01-engineering-quality-criteria.md`) e o red team que motivou G8. Prefixo `_` nos arquivos brutos Ã© intencional (agrupa antes de outros nomes em ordem alfabÃ©tica, sinaliza "transcript bruto, nÃ£o leia direto").
2. **ConvergÃªncia dos critÃ©rios por eixo** (`joint-review-criteria-round1-*`, `security-axis-criteria-round1-*`, `remaining-axes-round1-*`, `audit-areas-*`) â€” produziram `joint-review-criteria.md`.
3. **Full-audit dos 9 eixos formais** (`full-audit-round1-<eixo>-*`) â€” a rodada em andamento nesta sessÃ£o. PadrÃ£o de nomenclatura por eixo (`<eixo>` âˆˆ `arquitetura`, `qualidade`, `contexto`, `seguranca`, ...):
   - `full-audit-round1-<eixo>-claude.md`, `-claude-round2.md`, `-claude-round3.md`, ... â€” nota cega do Claude, uma por rodada.
   - `full-audit-round1-<eixo>-codex-prompt.txt` / `-codex-prompt-round2.txt`, ... â€” prompt enviado ao Codex CLI, um por rodada.
   - `full-audit-round1-<eixo>-codex-output-round1.txt`, `-round2.txt`, ... â€” saÃ­da bruta do Codex CLI. Se precisar do conteÃºdo, prefira o `-summary.md` correspondente, que jÃ¡ extrai a informaÃ§Ã£o sem o ruÃ­do de encoding.
   - `full-audit-round1-<eixo>-summary.md` â€” **o Ãºnico arquivo desta trinca que deveria ser lido diretamente.** Nota final por critÃ©rio (ambos os lados), achados corrigidos com commit real, achados restantes classificados em "impedimento externo real" vs. "escopo maior que correÃ§Ã£o pontual".

4. **Reabertura de um eixo jÃ¡ fechado** (`full-audit-round2-<eixo>-*`, mesmo padrÃ£o de nomenclatura acima, "round2" substitui "round1" no prefixo, nÃ£o sufixo `-round2.md` isolado como o de uma nota Claude dentro da mesma rodada) â€” usada quando a recomendaÃ§Ã£o de fechamento de um `full-audit-round1-<eixo>-summary.md` cita uma condiÃ§Ã£o objetiva de reabertura (ex.: "reabrir quando X for implementado") e essa condiÃ§Ã£o se materializa. Arquivos de transcript bruto (`.txt`, prefixo `_`, ou os `full-audit-*-codex-output-*.txt`) nunca sÃ£o fonte de verdade por si sÃ³ â€” sempre prefira o `.md` de resumo/nota que os acompanha. **PolÃ­tica de proveniÃªncia/metadata**: os `.md` de nota/resumo carregam frontmatter (`status`/`owner`/`authority`, mais `Last verified` nos routers); os `.txt` brutos nÃ£o â€” exigir frontmatter em cada transcript violaria proporcionalidade (`principles.md` #1) sem ganho real, jÃ¡ que sua proveniÃªncia Ã© herdada do `.md` que os acompanha (mesmo prefixo de nome) e da posiÃ§Ã£o cronolÃ³gica descrita nesta seÃ§Ã£o. Isso Ã© uma decisÃ£o de design, nÃ£o uma lacuna: um transcript nunca Ã© lido isoladamente por convenÃ§Ã£o (ver acima), entÃ£o nÃ£o precisa carregar sua prÃ³pria metadata.

## Contexto real (verificado no cÃ³digo antes de propor, nÃ£o sÃ³ a descriÃ§Ã£o da auditoria)

Arquivos: `src/modules/document-archive/domain/guest-session.ts`, `src/modules/document-archive/application/guest-document-access-service.ts`, `src/modules/document-archive/http/document-archive-guest-handlers.ts`.

O prÃ³prio `handleSubmitEvidence` jÃ¡ documenta o achado (comentÃ¡rio nas linhas 192-201): o cookie de sessÃ£o Ã© global por navegador (`__Host-et_docarchive_guest_session`, `Path=/`, sem escopo por token) - duas abas com dois links de guest diferentes compartilham o mesmo cookie jar; a segunda emissÃ£o de sessÃ£o sobrescreve o cookie da primeira aba, e a chamada `uploads` da primeira aba depois resolve contra a SEGUNDA sessÃ£o/DocumentRequest, nÃ£o a prÃ³pria. `requireToken(req)` no path Ã© chamado sÃ³ "for route symmetry/observability" - nunca usado pra resolver ou validar nada.

`GuestSession` (domÃ­nio) JÃ carrega os 2 campos que fecham esse gap sem exigir schema/migraÃ§Ã£o nova: `documentRequestId` (o request que a sessÃ£o representa) e `credentialSelectorHash` (o selectorHash do `RequestAccessCredential` que mintou esta sessÃ£o) - hoje documentado como "audit trail only, never used for authorization". `RequestAccessCredential`/`GuestSession` usam o mesmo esquema selector.secret + HMAC-SHA256(pepper) + `timingSafeEqual` (`request-access-credential.ts`/`guest-session.ts`).

## Pesquisa externa (E-014, decisÃ£o nÃ­vel 5-6 que redefine um padrÃ£o jÃ¡ resolvido fora deste projeto): SIM

VÃ­nculo de sessÃ£o ao recurso do path Ã© o padrÃ£o estabelecido de defesa contra IDOR/"confused deputy" em fluxos de link-por-e-mail/token: OWASP Testing Guide (WSTG-BUSL-04, "Testing for Process Timing"/IDOR) e OWASP Session Management Cheat Sheet recomendam nunca inferir o recurso-alvo de uma mutaÃ§Ã£o sÃ³ do estado de sessÃ£o ambiente quando a prÃ³pria URL/path jÃ¡ carrega um identificador de recurso - a mutaÃ§Ã£o deve reprovar a identidade do recurso do path contra a sessÃ£o a cada chamada, nÃ£o confiar que a sessÃ£o "deve" corresponder. Confirmando: https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Session_Management_Cheat_Sheet.html (acessado 2026-09-12) e https://owasp.org/www-community/Testing_for_Insecure_Direct_Object_References (mesma data). Checklist derivado (nasce nesta Rodada 1, nunca adicionado depois):

1. (peso 50%) **Toda mutaÃ§Ã£o que aceita um token de path/recurso explÃ­cito reprova esse token contra a sessÃ£o ambiente antes de agir** - nunca confia que "a sessÃ£o presente deve ser a certa".
2. (peso 30%) **A falha de vÃ­nculo colapsa no MESMO erro genÃ©rico de qualquer outra falha de guest-auth** (disciplina anti-enumeraÃ§Ã£o jÃ¡ estabelecida neste mÃ³dulo, Decision 4) - nunca um erro diferenciado "sessÃ£o nÃ£o pertence a este link".
3. (peso 20%) **O mecanismo de vÃ­nculo nÃ£o abre uma nova superfÃ­cie de timing/enumeraÃ§Ã£o** - comparaÃ§Ã£o de hash de selector nÃ£o-secreto nÃ£o precisa de `timingSafeEqual` (jÃ¡ nÃ£o Ã© feito hoje pra outros lookups de selectorHash neste mÃ³dulo), mas nÃ£o deve vazar qual dos dois (path ou sessÃ£o) estÃ¡ "errado".

## Proposta de desenho

**VÃ­nculo por `credentialSelectorHash`, nÃ£o por `documentRequestId` direto.** Duas opÃ§Ãµes Ã³bvias existiam - comparar `session.documentRequestId` contra o `documentRequestId` do credential do path, ou comparar os dois `selectorHash`. Escolho `credentialSelectorHash` porque Ã© o campo que jÃ¡ existe com EXATAMENTE esse propÃ³sito documentado ("the credential's own selectorHash this session was minted from") e evita uma segunda leitura completa do `DocumentRequest`/re-verificaÃ§Ã£o de secret do credential do path a cada chamada de mutaÃ§Ã£o - sÃ³ precisamos do SELECTOR (nÃ£o do secret) do token do path pra derivar o hash e comparar.

1. `handleSubmitEvidence`/`handleConfirmUpload` (HTTP): passam a repassar o `token` do path (jÃ¡ extraÃ­do por `requireToken`, hoje descartado) para o service, em vez de sÃ³ chamar `requireToken` por "simetria".
2. `GuestDocumentAccessService.submitEvidence`/`confirmUploadInFlight`: novo parÃ¢metro `pathToken: string`. ApÃ³s `resolveSession()`, antes de qualquer efeito colateral: `parseRequestAccessToken(pathToken)` (reaproveita o parser jÃ¡ existente, nunca um novo); se invÃ¡lido, `GuestAccessInvalidError` (mesmo colapso genÃ©rico). Calcula `hmacRequestAccessCrypto.hash(this.pepper, parsed.selector)` e compara (`===`, nÃ£o `timingSafeEqual` - nÃ£o Ã© comparaÃ§Ã£o de segredo, Ã© comparaÃ§Ã£o de identificador de recurso, mesma classe que comparar 2 UUIDs) contra `resolved.session.credentialSelectorHash`. Mismatch â†’ `GuestAccessInvalidError`, mesmo path de erro de qualquer outra falha.
3. **Nunca reautentica o secret do credential do path** - a sessÃ£o jÃ¡ provou posse na emissÃ£o (Decision 4); esta checagem sÃ³ prova "o path que o navegador estÃ¡ visitando agora Ã© o mesmo recurso que mintou a sessÃ£o presente no cookie", fechando exatamente o cenÃ¡rio de 2 abas da auditoria.
4. `handleGetGuestRequest`/`handleStartGuestSession`/`handleListGuestDocumentTypes` (rotas que resolvem sÃ³ pelo path token, nunca por sessÃ£o) nÃ£o precisam de mudanÃ§a - o problema Ã© exclusivo das 2 rotas que hoje resolvem pela sessÃ£o-cookie mas recebem um token de path que nunca comparam.

## Contexto real (verificado no código antes de propor, não só a descrição da auditoria)

Arquivos: `src/modules/document-archive/domain/guest-session.ts`, `src/modules/document-archive/application/guest-document-access-service.ts`, `src/modules/document-archive/http/document-archive-guest-handlers.ts`.

O próprio `handleSubmitEvidence` já documenta o achado (comentário nas linhas 192-201): o cookie de sessão é global por navegador (`__Host-et_docarchive_guest_session`, `Path=/`, sem escopo por token) - duas abas com dois links de guest diferentes compartilham o mesmo cookie jar; a segunda emissão de sessão sobrescreve o cookie da primeira aba, e a chamada `uploads` da primeira aba depois resolve contra a SEGUNDA sessão/DocumentRequest, não a própria. `requireToken(req)` no path é chamado só "for route symmetry/observability" - nunca usado pra resolver ou validar nada.

`GuestSession` (domínio) JÁ carrega os 2 campos que fecham esse gap sem exigir schema/migração nova: `documentRequestId` (o request que a sessão representa) e `credentialSelectorHash` (o selectorHash do `RequestAccessCredential` que mintou esta sessão) - hoje documentado como "audit trail only, never used for authorization". `RequestAccessCredential`/`GuestSession` usam o mesmo esquema selector.secret + HMAC-SHA256(pepper) + `timingSafeEqual` (`request-access-credential.ts`/`guest-session.ts`).

## Pesquisa externa (E-014, decisão nível 5-6 que redefine um padrão já resolvido fora deste projeto): SIM

Vínculo de sessão ao recurso do path é o padrão estabelecido de defesa contra IDOR/"confused deputy" em fluxos de link-por-e-mail/token: OWASP Testing Guide (WSTG-BUSL-04, "Testing for Process Timing"/IDOR) e OWASP Session Management Cheat Sheet recomendam nunca inferir o recurso-alvo de uma mutação só do estado de sessão ambiente quando a própria URL/path já carrega um identificador de recurso - a mutação deve reprovar a identidade do recurso do path contra a sessão a cada chamada, não confiar que a sessão "deve" corresponder. Confirmando: https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Session_Management_Cheat_Sheet.html (acessado 2026-09-12) e https://owasp.org/www-community/Testing_for_Insecure_Direct_Object_References (mesma data). Checklist derivado (nasce nesta Rodada 1, nunca adicionado depois):

1. (peso 50%) **Toda mutação que aceita um token de path/recurso explícito reprova esse token contra a sessão ambiente antes de agir** - nunca confia que "a sessão presente deve ser a certa".
2. (peso 30%) **A falha de vínculo colapsa no MESMO erro genérico de qualquer outra falha de guest-auth** (disciplina anti-enumeração já estabelecida neste módulo, Decision 4) - nunca um erro diferenciado "sessão não pertence a este link".
3. (peso 20%) **O mecanismo de vínculo não abre uma nova superfície de timing/enumeração** - comparação de hash de selector não-secreto não precisa de `timingSafeEqual` (já não é feito hoje pra outros lookups de selectorHash neste módulo), mas não deve vazar qual dos dois (path ou sessão) está "errado".

## Proposta de desenho

**Vínculo por `credentialSelectorHash`, não por `documentRequestId` direto.** Duas opções óbvias existiam - comparar `session.documentRequestId` contra o `documentRequestId` do credential do path, ou comparar os dois `selectorHash`. Escolho `credentialSelectorHash` porque é o campo que já existe com EXATAMENTE esse propósito documentado ("the credential's own selectorHash this session was minted from") e evita uma segunda leitura completa do `DocumentRequest`/re-verificação de secret do credential do path a cada chamada de mutação - só precisamos do SELECTOR (não do secret) do token do path pra derivar o hash e comparar.

1. `handleSubmitEvidence`/`handleConfirmUpload` (HTTP): passam a repassar o `token` do path (já extraído por `requireToken`, hoje descartado) para o service, em vez de só chamar `requireToken` por "simetria".
2. `GuestDocumentAccessService.submitEvidence`/`confirmUploadInFlight`: novo parâmetro `pathToken: string`. Após `resolveSession()`, antes de qualquer efeito colateral: `parseRequestAccessToken(pathToken)` (reaproveita o parser já existente, nunca um novo); se inválido, `GuestAccessInvalidError` (mesmo colapso genérico). Calcula `hmacRequestAccessCrypto.hash(this.pepper, parsed.selector)` e compara (`===`, não `timingSafeEqual` - não é comparação de segredo, é comparação de identificador de recurso, mesma classe que comparar 2 UUIDs) contra `resolved.session.credentialSelectorHash`. Mismatch → `GuestAccessInvalidError`, mesmo path de erro de qualquer outra falha.
3. **Nunca reautentica o secret do credential do path** - a sessão já provou posse na emissão (Decision 4); esta checagem só prova "o path que o navegador está visitando agora é o mesmo recurso que mintou a sessão presente no cookie", fechando exatamente o cenário de 2 abas da auditoria.
4. `handleGetGuestRequest`/`handleStartGuestSession`/`handleListGuestDocumentTypes` (rotas que resolvem só pelo path token, nunca por sessão) não precisam de mudança - o problema é exclusivo das 2 rotas que hoje resolvem pela sessão-cookie mas recebem um token de path que nunca comparam.

## Pergunta

O desenho fecha as 3 categorias do checklist? Algum achado bloqueante na abordagem (`credentialSelectorHash` vs. `documentRequestId`, ausência de `timingSafeEqual` na comparação, escopo das 2 rotas afetadas)?

   11:  *  2. `startGuestSession()` — the ONLY way a `GuestSession` is minted (Decision 4: never
   12:  *     automatically from the credential alone, an explicit human interstitial action every
   13:  *     time — see `guest-session.ts`'s doc comment for why).
   14:  *  3. `submitEvidence()` — validates a `GuestSession` token (same resolve discipline as the
   15:  *     credential) + CSRF double-submit + its OWN idempotency key (never the credential/session,
   16:  *     so a network retry can never double-create a DocumentVersion), then creates a brand-new
   17:  *     Document + DocumentVersion landing at RECEIVED (C2, `document-domain-functional-decisions.md`:
   18:  *     "Todo upload externo deve chegar como RECEIVED e depender de aceite interno" — guest
   19:  *     uploads must NEVER auto-accept) in one `TransactWriteItems`, fenced through
   20:  *     `executeTenantBusinessMutation` exactly like the subject-module precedent.
   21:  *
   22:  * Anti-enumeration discipline (Decision 4, mirrors `GuestTokenInvalidError`): every failure mode
   23:  * of every one of the three entry points — malformed/nonexistent/wrong-secret/expired/revoked/
   24:  * rate-limited/CSRF-mismatch — collapses to the SAME `GuestAccessInvalidError`, with no `details`
   25:  * populated (`AppError.toJSON()` serializes `details` into the HTTP response).
   26:  */
   27: import { AppError, ValidationError, TenantNotActiveError } from "../../../shared/errors/app-error.js";
   28: import { authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity, type AuthorizedTenantId } from "../../identity/domain/authorization.js";
   29: import { buildExistenceConditionCheck, buildVersionedCreate, buildVersionedUpdate, isTransactionCanceled, type EntityKey } from "../../../shared/dynamodb/occ.js";
   30: import { documentTypeKey, type DocumentType } from "../domain/document-type.js";
   31: import { executeTenantBusinessMutation } from "../../../shared/tenant-lifecycle/tenant-business-mutation.js";
   32: import type { UploadUrlSigner } from "../../document/ports/upload-url-signer.js";
   33: import { MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES } from "../../document/application/upload-validation.js";
   34: import {
   35:   buildDocumentArchiveQuarantineKey,
   36:   deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue,
   37:   documentFileGsi8Keys,
   38:   documentFileKey,
   39:   FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   40:   isNonTerminalFileScanStatus,
   41:   type DocumentFile,
   42: } from "../domain/document-file.js";
   43: import { MAX_OCC_RETRIES } from "./apply-file-scan-result.js";
   44: import { defaultStorageQuota, storageQuotaKey, wouldExceedStorageQuota, type TenantStorageQuota } from "../domain/storage-quota.js";
   45: import {
   46:   epochSecondsFromIso,
   47:   hmacRequestAccessCrypto,
   48:   issueRequestAccessCredential,
   49:   parseRequestAccessToken,
   50:   requestAccessCredentialKey,
   51:   requestAccessSecretMatches,
   52:   type IssuedRequestAccessCredential,
   53:   type RequestAccessCredential,
   54: } from "../domain/request-access-credential.js";
   55: import {
   56:   GUEST_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS,
   57:   guestSessionCsrfMatches,
   58:   guestSessionKey,
   59:   guestSessionSecretMatches,
   60:   hmacGuestSessionCrypto,
   61:   issueGuestSession,
   62:   parseGuestSessionToken,
   63:   type GuestSession,
   64:   type IssuedGuestSession,
   65: } from "../domain/guest-session.js";
   66: import { documentRequestKey, isDocumentRequestLive, type DocumentRequest } from "../domain/document-request.js";
   67: import { requirementKey } from "../domain/requirement.js";
   68: import { trackedSubjectKeyForFence } from "../domain/requirement-template.js";
   69: import { documentKey, documentGsi1Keys, documentGsi2Keys, type Document } from "../domain/document.js";
   70: import { documentVersionKey, reviewQueueGsi5Keys, type DocumentVersion } from "../domain/document-version.js";
   71: import { documentVersionEventKey, type DocumentVersionEvent } from "../domain/document-version-event.js";
   72: import type { DocumentArchiveStore } from "../ports/document-archive-store.js";
   73: import type { DocumentArchiveIdGenerator } from "./id-generator.js";
   74: import type { DocumentArchiveGuestRateLimiter } from "./document-archive-guest-rate-limiter.js";
   75: 
   76: /** Single generic error for every guest-auth/CSRF/rate-limit failure mode (Decision 4). */
   77: export class GuestAccessInvalidError extends AppError {
   78:   constructor() {
   79:     super({ code: "GUEST_ACCESS_INVALID", category: "AUTH", message: "Invalid, expired, or unauthorized guest access.", retryable: false });
   80:     this.name = "GuestAccessInvalidError";
   81:   }
   82: }
   83: 
   84: const RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE = 30;
   85: const RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS = 60;
   86: 
   87: // ADR-0013 (D-265) — same allowlist/size ceiling as `document`/M6's own
   88: // `ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES`/`MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES` (`document-service.ts`), ported as a literal (not
   89: // cross-imported as a Set) rather than reused, same "no cross-module coupling on an internal
   90: // constant shape" precedent `storage-quota.ts`'s module doc comment already establishes —
   91: // `MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES` itself IS imported (a plain number, not a module-internal shape).
   92: const ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(["application/pdf", "image/jpeg", "image/png"]);
   93: 
   94: // 10 minutes — same window `document-archive-service.ts`'s `PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS` uses, reusing
   95: // `FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` rather than a second constant (D-179 slice 3 precedent: presign
   96: // window and scan-timeout deadline must never drift apart).
   97: const PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS = FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS;
   98: // ADR-0013 Rodada 3 — small margin so a presigned URL is never issued expiring at the exact
   99: // instant the reconciliation worker could already declare TIMEOUT.
  100: const PRESIGN_SAFETY_MARGIN_SECONDS = 5;
  101: 
  102: export interface GuestDocumentAccessServiceDeps {
  103:   store: DocumentArchiveStore;
  104:   tableName: string;
  105:   ids: DocumentArchiveIdGenerator;
  106:   rateLimiter: DocumentArchiveGuestRateLimiter;
  107:   pepper: string;
  108:   /** ADR-0013 (D-265) — the SAME quarantine bucket/signer `DocumentArchiveService` already
  109:    * depends on (`document-archive-service.ts`'s own deps doc comment) — no new bucket, no new
  110:    * signer abstraction, only a second call site against the existing ones. */
  111:   quarantineBucket: string;
  112:   signer: UploadUrlSigner;
  113:   now?: () => string;
  114: }
  115: 
  116: export interface ResolvedCredential {
  117:   credential: RequestAccessCredential;
  118:   request: DocumentRequest;
  119:   /** G02 (Block 6, D-2xx) — the guest wizard's own card copy ("{Fornecedor} solicitou evidência
  120:    * para o requisito: {Requisito}") needs a human-readable name, never the opaque
  121:    * subjectId/requirementId this service otherwise deals in exclusively. Best-effort/absent-safe
  122:    * (`?`): a Subject/Requirement deleted after the credential was issued must never turn an
  123:    * otherwise-valid guest session into a hard failure — the UI degrades to omitting the name,
  124:    * same "no fabricated value" discipline as the rest of this module. */
  125:   subjectDisplayName?: string;
  126:   requirementName?: string;
  127: }
  128: 
  129: export interface ResolvedSession {
  130:   session: GuestSession;
  131:   request: DocumentRequest;
  132: }
  133: 
  134: export interface IssueCredentialInput {
  135:   tenantId: string;
  136:   subjectId: string;
  137:   requirementId: string;
  138:   documentRequestId: string;
  139:   /** TTL for the credential — must equal the business Request's deadline (Decision 4). Callers
  140:    * (the future recurrence/DocumentRequest-creation flow) always supply this explicitly; this
  141:    * service never invents a default TTL. */
  142:   expiresAt: string;
  143: }
  144: 
  145: export interface StartGuestSessionResult {
  146:   session: IssuedGuestSession;
  147:   expiresAt: string;
  148:   /** G02 (Block 6, D-2xx) — see `ResolvedCredential`'s own doc comment; startGuestSession is the
  149:    * ONE call G02 actually makes on page load (it never calls the bare layer-1 GET route
  150:    * directly, see `document-archive-guest-handlers.ts`'s header comment), so this is the single
  151:    * place the wizard's card copy gets its display names from. */
  152:   subjectDisplayName?: string;
  153:   requirementName?: string;
  154: }
  155: 
  156: export interface SubmitEvidenceInput {
  157:   fileName: string;
  158:   /** Mandatory since D-243 (clean cutover, no coexistence with the old free-text `documentType`
  159:    * field D-184 had left optional) — always validated against the tenant's DocumentType catalog
  160:    * via the transactional ConditionCheck below. */
  161:   documentTypeId: string;
  162:   /** ADR-0013 (D-265) — real upload metadata, validated against the same allowlist/size ceiling
  163:    * as the authenticated path (`ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES`/`MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES` above). Any validation
  164:    * failure collapses into the generic `GuestAccessInvalidError`, never a differentiated message
  165:    * (anti-enumeration discipline, module header comment). */
  166:   mediaType: string;
  167:   contentLength: number;
  168:   checksumSha256: string;
  169:   /** Idempotency key OWNED by this call, distinct from the credential/session token — a network
  170:    * retry replaying the same key must never double-create a DocumentVersion. */
  171:   idempotencyKey: string;
  172: }
  173: 
  174: export interface SubmitEvidenceResult {
  175:   documentId: string;
  176:   versionId: string;
  177:   seq: number;
  178:   /** ADR-0013 (D-265) — needed so `confirmUploadInFlight()` can resolve this submission's
  179:    * `DocumentFile` from the idempotency record alone (never a raw client-supplied `fileId`). */
  180:   fileId: string;
  181:   /** Present only while the file is still `PENDING_UPLOAD` and inside its presign window — see
  182:    * `computeUploadOffer()`. Absent means "nothing more to do for this submission" (already
  183:    * uploaded, expired, or otherwise terminal) — never distinguished further, same anti-
  184:    * enumeration collapse as the rest of this module. */
  185:   uploadUrl?: string;
  186:   requiredHeaders?: Record<string, string>;
  187: }
  188: 
  189: /** Deliberately minimal — never exposes `metadataFields`/`status`/timestamps/`version` to a
  190:  * guest, only what a submission UI needs to populate a `documentType` choice. */
  191: export interface GuestDocumentTypeSummary {
  192:   documentTypeId: string;
  193:   displayName: string;
  194: }
  195: 
  196: export class GuestDocumentAccessService {
  197:   private readonly store: DocumentArchiveStore;
  198:   private readonly tableName: string;
  199:   private readonly ids: DocumentArchiveIdGenerator;
  200:   private readonly rateLimiter: DocumentArchiveGuestRateLimiter;
  201:   private readonly pepper: string;
  202:   private readonly quarantineBucket: string;
  203:   private readonly signer: UploadUrlSigner;
  204:   private readonly now: () => string;
  205: 
  206:   constructor(deps: GuestDocumentAccessServiceDeps) {
  207:     this.store = deps.store;
  208:     this.tableName = deps.tableName;
  209:     this.ids = deps.ids;
  210:     this.rateLimiter = deps.rateLimiter;
  211:     this.pepper = deps.pepper;
  212:     this.quarantineBucket = deps.quarantineBucket;
  213:     this.signer = deps.signer;
  214:     this.now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
  215:   }
  216: 
  217:   /** Issuance entry point — normally invoked by an authenticated internal flow when a
  218:    * DocumentRequest is created (recurrence's job, D-143 Decision 8, a separate follow-up task).
  219:    * Kept here as the minimal, explicit extension point this task needs: guest access cannot be
  220:    * exercised end to end without SOME way to mint a credential. */
  221:   async issueCredential(input: IssueCredentialInput): Promise<IssuedRequestAccessCredential> {
  222:     const issued = issueRequestAccessCredential(this.pepper);
  223:     const now = this.now();
  224:     const credential: RequestAccessCredential = {
  225:       ...requestAccessCredentialKey(issued.selectorHash),
  226:       entityType: "RequestAccessCredential",
  227:       selectorHash: issued.selectorHash,
  228:       secretHash: issued.secretHash,
  229:       tenantId: input.tenantId,
  230:       subjectId: input.subjectId,
  231:       requirementId: input.requirementId,
  232:       documentRequestId: input.documentRequestId,
  233:       tokenVersion: 1,
  234:       expiresAt: input.expiresAt,
  235:       purgeAfterTtl: epochSecondsFromIso(input.expiresAt),
  236:       createdAt: now,
  237:       updatedAt: now,
  238:       version: 1,
  239:     };
  240:     const created = await this.store.putIfAbsent(credential);
  241:     if (!created) {
  242:       // Astronomically unlikely selector collision (128 bits) — never silently overwrite an
  243:       // existing credential pointer.
  244:       throw new ValidationError("Could not issue credential (selector collision).");
  245:     }
  246:     return issued;
  247:   }
  248: 
  249:   /** Layer 1: validates a `RequestAccessCredential` token end to end. Never mints a session —
  250:    * callers that only need to SHOW request info (an interstitial page) call this alone. */
  251:   async resolveCredential(rawToken: string, requestContext: { ip: string }): Promise<ResolvedCredential> {
  252:     const parsed = parseRequestAccessToken(rawToken);
  253:     if (!parsed) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  254: 
  255:     const selectorHash = hmacRequestAccessCrypto.hash(this.pepper, parsed.selector);
  256: 
  257:     // Rate limit consumed BEFORE lookup, by both the selector (computable even if no pointer
  258:     // exists) and IP — Decision 4's multidimensional requirement, applied at the earliest point
  259:     // an oracle could otherwise form (same discipline as GuestSubmissionService.resolveToken).
  260:     try {
  261:       await this.rateLimiter.consumeBoth({ requestKey: selectorHash, ip: requestContext.ip, limit: RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE, windowSeconds: RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS });
  262:     } catch {
  263:       throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  264:     }
  265: 
  266:     const pointer = await this.store.get<RequestAccessCredential>(requestAccessCredentialKey(selectorHash));
  267: 
  268:     // Dummy anti-timing path — even when no pointer exists, still compute a hash and a
  269:     // timingSafeEqual comparison so response time never depends on selector existence. The dummy
  270:     // hash's fixed prefix can never collide with a real secretHash (issueRequestAccessCredential
  271:     // never produces this literal input).
  272:     const targetSecretHash = pointer?.secretHash ?? hmacRequestAccessCrypto.hash(this.pepper, `dummy:${selectorHash}`);
  273:     const secretOk = requestAccessSecretMatches(this.pepper, parsed.secret, targetSecretHash);
  274:     if (!pointer || !secretOk) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  275: 
  276:     if (pointer.revokedAt) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  277:     if (pointer.expiresAt < this.now()) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  278: 
  279:     const request = await this.store.get<DocumentRequest>(documentRequestKey(authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(pointer), pointer.subjectId, pointer.documentRequestId));
  280:     if (!request || !isDocumentRequestLive(request.status)) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  281:     // Re-validate against the live Request's deadline too (same "credential TTL OR deadline,
  282:     // whichever is sooner, re-checked at resolve time" discipline as GuestTokenPointer's D-037
  283:     // precedent) — catches a deadline edited after the credential was issued without reissuing it.
  284:     if (request.deadline && request.deadline < this.now()) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  285: 
  286:     if (request.status === "REQUESTED") await this.markOpened(request);
  287: 
  288:     const tenantId = authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(pointer);
  289:     const [subject, requirement] = await Promise.all([
  290:       this.store.get<EntityKey & { displayName?: string }>(trackedSubjectKeyForFence(tenantId, pointer.subjectId)),
  291:       this.store.get<EntityKey & { name?: string }>(requirementKey(tenantId, pointer.subjectId, pointer.requirementId)),
  292:     ]);
  293: 
  294:     return { credential: pointer, request, subjectDisplayName: subject?.displayName, requirementName: requirement?.name };
  295:   }
  296: 
  297:   /**
  298:    * Discovery route (item 6 of D-173's estado-final-consolidado.md, engineering-only slice —
  299:    * see D-19x for the full contradiction/pendency writeup): lets a guest who has a valid
  300:    * credential token discover which `DocumentTypeId`s are currently valid to submit, WITHOUT any
  301:    * `authorize()`/`RequestContext` — same token-resolution discipline as `resolveCredential()`,
  302:    * reused here rather than duplicated. Only ACTIVE types are ever returned (never DRAFT/
  303:    * DEPRECATED) — a guest has no legitimate reason to see a type it cannot successfully submit
  304:    * against, since D-184's transactional ConditionCheck would reject anything else anyway.
  305:    * Read-only, no side effect (does NOT call `markOpened` — that is `resolveCredential`'s own
  306:    * concern and this route can be polled/refreshed by a guest UI without mutating Request state).
  307:    */
  308:   async listActiveDocumentTypesForGuest(rawToken: string, requestContext: { ip: string }): Promise<GuestDocumentTypeSummary[]> {
  309:     const parsed = parseRequestAccessToken(rawToken);
  310:     if (!parsed) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  311: 
  312:     const selectorHash = hmacRequestAccessCrypto.hash(this.pepper, parsed.selector);
  313:     try {
  314:       await this.rateLimiter.consumeBoth({ requestKey: selectorHash, ip: requestContext.ip, limit: RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE, windowSeconds: RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS });
  315:     } catch {
  316:       throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  317:     }
  318: 
  319:     const pointer = await this.store.get<RequestAccessCredential>(requestAccessCredentialKey(selectorHash));
  320:     const targetSecretHash = pointer?.secretHash ?? hmacRequestAccessCrypto.hash(this.pepper, `dummy:${selectorHash}`);
  321:     const secretOk = requestAccessSecretMatches(this.pepper, parsed.secret, targetSecretHash);
  322:     if (!pointer || !secretOk) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  323:     if (pointer.revokedAt) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  324:     if (pointer.expiresAt < this.now()) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  325: 
  326:     const request = await this.store.get<DocumentRequest>(documentRequestKey(authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(pointer), pointer.subjectId, pointer.documentRequestId));
  327:     if (!request || !isDocumentRequestLive(request.status)) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  328:     if (request.deadline && request.deadline < this.now()) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  329: 
  330:     // Same GSI1 DOCTYPESTATUS namespace as DocumentArchiveService.listDocumentTypes, read
  331:     // directly against the store (never through that authorize()-gated method — this whole
  332:     // module never calls authorize()/RequestContextResolver, see file header). Catalogs are
  333:     // small (a handful to low hundreds of types per tenant), so — unlike the internal
  334:     // paginated-by-design admin listing — this accumulates every page here: a guest-facing
  335:     // discovery list has no caller-driven cursor to hand back, and a hard page cap (`MAX_PAGES`)
  336:     // bounds the cost of a pathological tenant rather than looping unbounded.
  337:     const MAX_PAGES = 20;
  338:     const items: DocumentType[] = [];
  339:     let exclusiveStartKey: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  340:     for (let page = 0; page < MAX_PAGES; page++) {
  341:       const result = await this.store.queryIndexPage<DocumentType>({
  342:         indexName: "GSI1",
  343:         partitionKeyValue: `TENANT#${pointer.tenantId}#DOCTYPESTATUS#ACTIVE`,
  344:         exclusiveStartKey,
  345:       });
  346:       items.push(...result.items);
  347:       if (!result.lastEvaluatedKey) break;
  348:       exclusiveStartKey = result.lastEvaluatedKey;
  349:     }
  350: 
  351:     return items.map((item) => ({ documentTypeId: item.documentTypeId, displayName: item.displayName }));
  352:   }
  353: 
  354:   /** Layer 2: the ONLY way a GuestSession is minted — always an explicit call, never a side
  355:    * effect of `resolveCredential`. */
  356:   async startGuestSession(rawToken: string, requestContext: { ip: string }): Promise<StartGuestSessionResult> {
  357:     const resolved = await this.resolveCredential(rawToken, requestContext);
  358:     const issued = issueGuestSession(this.pepper);
  359:     const now = this.now();
  360:     const expiresAt = new Date(Date.parse(now) + GUEST_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS * 1000).toISOString();
  361:     const session: GuestSession = {
  362:       ...guestSessionKey(issued.selectorHash),
  363:       entityType: "GuestSession",
  364:       selectorHash: issued.selectorHash,
  365:       secretHash: issued.secretHash,
  366:       tenantId: resolved.credential.tenantId,
  367:       subjectId: resolved.credential.subjectId,
  368:       requirementId: resolved.credential.requirementId,
  369:       documentRequestId: resolved.credential.documentRequestId,
  370:       credentialSelectorHash: resolved.credential.selectorHash,
  371:       csrfTokenHash: issued.csrfTokenHash,
  372:       expiresAt,
  373:       purgeAfterTtl: epochSecondsFromIso(expiresAt),
  374:       createdAt: now,
  375:       updatedAt: now,
  376:       version: 1,
  377:     };
  378:     const created = await this.store.putIfAbsent(session);
  379:     if (!created) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError(); // astronomically unlikely selector collision.
  380:     return { session: issued, expiresAt, subjectDisplayName: resolved.subjectDisplayName, requirementName: resolved.requirementName };
  381:   }
  382: 
  383:   /** Resolves a GuestSession token — same parse/rate-limit/lookup/dummy-compare/expiry
  384:    * discipline as `resolveCredential`, over the session's own tenantless namespace. */
  385:   private async resolveSession(rawSessionToken: string, requestContext: { ip: string }): Promise<ResolvedSession> {
  386:     const parsed = parseGuestSessionToken(rawSessionToken);
  387:     if (!parsed) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  388: 
  389:     const selectorHash = hmacGuestSessionCrypto.hash(this.pepper, parsed.selector);
  390:     try {
  391:       await this.rateLimiter.consumeBoth({ requestKey: selectorHash, ip: requestContext.ip, limit: RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE, windowSeconds: RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS });
  392:     } catch {
  393:       throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  394:     }
  395: 
  396:     const pointer = await this.store.get<GuestSession>(guestSessionKey(selectorHash));
  397:     const targetSecretHash = pointer?.secretHash ?? hmacGuestSessionCrypto.hash(this.pepper, `dummy:${selectorHash}`);
  398:     const secretOk = guestSessionSecretMatches(this.pepper, parsed.secret, targetSecretHash);
  399:     if (!pointer || !secretOk) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  400:     if (pointer.expiresAt < this.now()) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  401: 
  402:     const request = await this.store.get<DocumentRequest>(documentRequestKey(authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(pointer), pointer.subjectId, pointer.documentRequestId));
  403:     if (!request || !isDocumentRequestLive(request.status)) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  404: 
  405:     return { session: pointer, request };
  406:   }
  407: 
  408:   /** Double-submit CSRF check shared by `submitEvidence()`/`confirmUploadInFlight()` (Codex
  409:    * review round 1, D-265 implementation: this was copy-pasted between the two before, real
  410:    * drift risk the ADR's own "reused, not duplicated" intent named). Cookie and header must both
  411:    * be present AND equal AND match the session's own stored hash (defends both "no cookie at
  412:    * all" and "attacker can set an arbitrary matching pair but doesn't know the real session's
  413:    * csrfToken" — the latter is the whole point of hashing it server-side rather than only
  414:    * comparing cookie===header). */
  415:   private assertCsrf(requestContext: { csrfCookieValue: string | undefined; csrfHeaderValue: string | undefined }, session: GuestSession): void {
  416:     if (!requestContext.csrfCookieValue || !requestContext.csrfHeaderValue || requestContext.csrfCookieValue !== requestContext.csrfHeaderValue) {
  417:       throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  418:     }
  419:     if (!guestSessionCsrfMatches(this.pepper, requestContext.csrfHeaderValue, session.csrfTokenHash)) {
  420:       throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  421:     }
  422:   }
  423: 
  424:   /** Layer 3: idempotent evidence submission. `csrfCookieValue`/`csrfHeaderValue` are the raw
  425:    * double-submit pair — mismatch (missing either side, or the two differing) is a CSRF failure
  426:    * and collapses into the same generic error as every other guest-auth failure mode.
  427:    *
  428:    * ADR-0013 (D-265) — now persists a real `DocumentFile` (PENDING_UPLOAD) in the same
  429:    * transaction and returns a presigned PUT, reusing the exact `DocumentFile`/scan pipeline
  430:    * already `APPROVED`/in production for `document`/`subject`/`document-archive` (D-163). Never
  431:    * activates the STARTER/PROMOTER gate (D-193) — inherited unchanged, a guest file follows the
  432:    * identical CLEAN-eligibility path an authenticated upload already does today. */
  433:   async submitEvidence(
  434:     rawSessionToken: string,
  435:     requestContext: { ip: string; csrfCookieValue: string | undefined; csrfHeaderValue: string | undefined },
  436:     input: SubmitEvidenceInput,
  437:   ): Promise<SubmitEvidenceResult> {
  438:     const resolved = await this.resolveSession(rawSessionToken, requestContext);
  439:     this.assertCsrf(requestContext, resolved.session);
  440: 
  441:     const tenantId = authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(resolved.session);
  442:     const subjectId = resolved.session.subjectId;
  443: 
  444:     const idempotencyKey = { PK: `TENANT#${tenantId}#SUBJECT#${subjectId}`, SK: `DOCREQUEST#${resolved.request.documentRequestId}#SUBMIT#${input.idempotencyKey}` };
  445:     // D-243 replay policy (payload-agnostic, first-write-wins, preserves D-143 Decision 4/D-184
  446:     // unchanged): this short-circuit runs BEFORE `documentTypeId` is ever read/validated below —
  447:     // a replay with the same key and a DIFFERENT `documentTypeId` still returns the original
  448:     // snapshot untouched, never re-validating or comparing the two values. ADR-0013 (Rodada 3):
  449:     // a replay also re-derives the upload offer fresh (never caches a stale `uploadUrl`) — the
  450:     // presign window is bounded by the FILE's own GSI8 deadline, never reopened.
  451:     const existingReplay = await this.store.get<{ resultSnapshot: SubmitEvidenceResult } & EntityKey>(idempotencyKey);
  452:     if (existingReplay) return this.withFreshUploadOffer(tenantId, existingReplay.resultSnapshot);
  453: 
  454:     // ADR-0013 (D-265) — validated in this fixed order (mediaType -> contentLength ->
  455:     // checksumSha256), any failure collapses to the generic guest error, never reaching the
  456:     // transaction below. Schema-level constraints (`docarchive-guest-submit-evidence-request.v1.json`)
  457:     // already enforce the same allowlist/range/pattern before this is reached — this is
  458:     // defense-in-depth at the service boundary, same "double-track" discipline the schema's own
  459:     // comment documents.
  460:     if (!ALLOWED_MEDIA_TYPES.has(input.mediaType)) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  461:     // Codex review round 1 (D-265 implementation): `Number.isInteger` closes a defense-in-depth
  462:     // gap the schema alone already covers (fractional/NaN `contentLength`) but this service-level
  463:     // check didn't — the schema is the HTTP boundary, this is the belt-and-suspenders re-check.
  464:     if (!Number.isInteger(input.contentLength) || input.contentLength <= 0 || input.contentLength > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  465:     if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(input.checksumSha256)) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  466: 
  467:     const now = this.now();
  468:     const documentId = this.ids.newDocumentId();
  469:     const versionId = this.ids.newVersionId();
  470:     const fileId = this.ids.newFileId();
  471:     // D-243 (supersedes D-184's conditional guard): `documentTypeId` is now mandatory end to end —
  472:     // the HTTP schema already rejects a missing/renamed field with 400 before this is reached, and
  473:     // the old free-text `documentType ?? requirementId` fallback is removed entirely (no coexistence,
  474:     // clean cutover). Always validated against the tenant's DocumentType catalog via the
  475:     // unconditional ConditionCheck below.
  476:     const documentTypeId = input.documentTypeId;
  477: 
  478:     const document: Document = {
  479:       ...documentKey(tenantId, documentId),
  480:       entityType: "Document",
  481:       documentId,
  482:       tenantId,
  483:       subjectId,
  484:       documentTypeId,
  485:       status: "ACTIVE",
  486:       hasValidity: false,
  487:       createdAt: now,
  488:       updatedAt: now,
  489:       version: 1,
  490:       ...documentGsi1Keys(tenantId, "ACTIVE", now, documentId),
  491:       // GSI2 (Documents-by-Subject) — a second attribute set on the same physical row, no
  492:       // mirror item needed (same pattern as DocumentArchiveService.createDocument).
  493:       ...documentGsi2Keys(tenantId, subjectId, documentTypeId, documentId),
  494:     };
  495: 
  496:     // ADR-0013 (D-265), reusing `reserveFiles()`'s exact GSI8/quarantine-key construction —
  497:     // never a second convention for the same concept.
  498:     const due = deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue({ scanStatus: "PENDING_UPLOAD", createdAt: now })!;
  499:     const file: DocumentFile = {
  500:       ...documentFileKey(tenantId, documentId, 1, fileId),
  501:       entityType: "DocumentFile",
  502:       tenantId,
  503:       documentId,
  504:       versionId,
  505:       seq: 1,
  506:       fileId,
  507:       role: "PRINCIPAL",
  508:       scanStatus: "PENDING_UPLOAD",
  509:       mediaType: input.mediaType,
  510:       contentLength: input.contentLength,
  511:       checksumSha256: input.checksumSha256,
  512:       quarantineObject: { bucket: this.quarantineBucket, key: buildDocumentArchiveQuarantineKey(tenantId, documentId, 1, fileId), versionId: "" },
  513:       createdAt: now,
  514:       updatedAt: now,
  515:       version: 1,
  516:       ...documentFileGsi8Keys({ dueAtIso: due.dueAtIso, tenantId, fileId }),
  517:     };
  518: 
  519:     const version: DocumentVersion = {
  520:       ...documentVersionKey(tenantId, documentId, 1),
  521:       entityType: "DocumentVersion",
  522:       versionId,
  523:       documentId,
  524:       tenantId,
  525:       seq: 1,
  526:       // C2 (document-domain-functional-decisions.md): every guest upload lands as RECEIVED,
  527:       // never auto-accepted — this single transaction goes straight to RECEIVED (the compressed
  528:       // internal-flow precedent for "DRAFT -> RECEIVED in one step", never further to ACCEPTED).
  529:       state: "RECEIVED",
  530:       origin: "GUEST_UPLOAD",
  531:       receivedAt: now,
  532:       // ADR-0013 (D-265) — the file set is sealed at creation (a guest submission is always
  533:       // exactly one PRINCIPAL file, never a batch), mirroring `reserveFiles()`'s literal-Put
  534:       // discipline: these fields are NEVER a second `Update` on the same item within one
  535:       // transaction (DynamoDB would reject two actions against the same key).
  536:       fileSetSealed: true,
  537:       principalFileId: fileId,
  538:       totalFiles: 1,
  539:       pendingFileScans: 1,
  540:       infectedFileScans: 0,
  541:       requestId: resolved.request.documentRequestId,
  542:       createdAt: now,
  543:       updatedAt: now,
  544:       version: 1,
  545:       ...reviewQueueGsi5Keys(tenantId, "RECEIVED", now, versionId),
  546:     };
  547: 
  548:     const event: DocumentVersionEvent = {
  549:       ...documentVersionEventKey(tenantId, documentId, 1, this.ids.newEventId()),
  550:       entityType: "DocumentVersionEvent",
  551:       tenantId,
  552:       documentId,
  553:       versionId,
  554:       type: "RECEIVED",
  555:       toState: "RECEIVED",
  556:       actor: `guest:${resolved.request.documentRequestId}`,
  557:       occurredAt: now,
  558:     };
  559: 
  560:     const result: SubmitEvidenceResult = { documentId, versionId, seq: 1, fileId };
  561:     const idempotencyRecord = {
  562:       ...idempotencyKey,
  563:       entityType: "IdempotencyRecord" as const,
  564:       tenantId,
  565:       payloadHash: `submitEvidence:${resolved.request.documentRequestId}:${input.idempotencyKey}`,
  566:       resultSnapshot: result,
  567:       createdAt: now,
  568:     };
  569: 
  570:     // storage-quota-scoping (D-249) / ADR-0013 (D-265) — same reservation-time capacity hold
  571:     // `reserveFiles()` takes, extended to the guest path (a caller-free upload target that
  572:     // previously never consumed quota at all — this change closes that abuse vector as a direct
  573:     // consequence of storing real bytes). In-memory check first for a clear early collapse;
  574:     // the transactional `Update` below is what actually serializes concurrent guest submissions.
  575:     const quota = await this.ensureStorageQuota(tenantId);
  576:     if (wouldExceedStorageQuota(quota, input.contentLength)) {
  577:       // Never a differentiated QuotaExceededError here (that's the authenticated-path 429) — a
  578:       // guest must never learn the tenant's storage state, same anti-enumeration collapse as
  579:       // every other failure mode this module has.
  580:       throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  581:     }
  582: 
  583:     const entries = [
  584:       // D-243: unconditional as of this decision (D-184's presence guard is removed — always
  585:       // required, always validated). Stays at position [0] (same `buildExistenceConditionCheck`/
  586:       // `documentTypeKey` as D-175/D-184); TOCTOU-safe by the same transaction, anti-enumeration
  587:       // `catch` below collapses nonexistent/`DEPRECATED` to the same generic guest error.
  588:       buildExistenceConditionCheck({ tableName: this.tableName, key: documentTypeKey(tenantId, documentTypeId), extra: { status: "ACTIVE" } }),
  589:       { Put: buildVersionedCreate(this.tableName, document as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey) },
  590:       { Put: buildVersionedCreate(this.tableName, version as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey) },
  591:       { Put: buildVersionedCreate(this.tableName, file as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey) },
  592:       { Put: buildVersionedCreate(this.tableName, event as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey) },
  593:       { Put: buildVersionedCreate(this.tableName, idempotencyRecord as unknown as Record<string, unknown> & EntityKey) },
  594:       {
  595:         Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
  596:           tableName: this.tableName,
  597:           key: documentRequestKey(tenantId, subjectId, resolved.request.documentRequestId),
  598:           tenantId,
  599:           expectedVersion: resolved.request.version,
  600:           set: { status: "SUBMITTED", lastSubmissionId: versionId, submissionCount: resolved.request.submissionCount + 1 },
  601:           now,
  602:         }),
  603:       },
  604:       {
  605:         Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
  606:           tableName: this.tableName,
  607:           key: storageQuotaKey(tenantId),
  608:           tenantId,
  609:           expectedVersion: quota.version,
  610:           set: { reservedBytes: quota.reservedBytes + input.contentLength },
  611:           now,
  612:           extraConditions: [
  613:             {
  614:               expression: "#used + #reserved + :requested <= #limit",
  615:               names: { "#used": "usedBytes", "#reserved": "reservedBytes", "#limit": "limitBytes" },
  616:               values: { ":requested": input.contentLength },
  617:             },
  618:           ],
  619:         }),
  620:       },
  621:     ];
  622: 
  623:     try {
  624:       await executeTenantBusinessMutation({ store: this.store, tableName: this.tableName, tenantId, entries });
  625:     } catch (err) {
  626:       if (isTransactionCanceled(err)) {
  627:         const replay = await this.store.get<{ resultSnapshot: SubmitEvidenceResult } & EntityKey>(idempotencyKey);
  628:         if (replay) return this.withFreshUploadOffer(tenantId, replay.resultSnapshot);
  629:         // A genuine race (e.g. two near-simultaneous submits of the same session) or the tenant
  630:         // leaving ACTIVE — the guest never sees which; same anti-enumeration collapse as every
  631:         // other failure mode on this surface.
  632:         throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  633:       }
  634:       if (err instanceof TenantNotActiveError) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
  635:       throw err;
  636:     }
  637: 
  638:     return this.withFreshUploadOffer(tenantId, result);
  639:   }
  640: 
  641:   /** ADR-0013 (D-265) — re-reads the `DocumentFile` fresh and computes whether a presigned PUT
  642:    * should still be offered, never trusting an in-memory copy from before the transaction
  643:    * committed (mirrors `applyFileScanResult()`'s own "always re-read" discipline). */
  644:   private async withFreshUploadOffer(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId, result: SubmitEvidenceResult): Promise<SubmitEvidenceResult> {
  645:     const file = await this.store.get<DocumentFile>(documentFileKey(tenantId, result.documentId, result.seq, result.fileId));
  646:     if (!file) return result;
  647:     return { ...result, ...(await this.computeUploadOffer(file)) };
  648:   }
  649: 
  650:   /** ADR-0013 (D-265), Rodada 3 — presign contract: present = "send the bytes now, the record
  651:    * still accepts them"; absent = "nothing more to do for this submission" (already uploaded,
  652:    * SCANNING, or terminal) — never distinguished further to the guest. Never presigns past the
  653:    * file's own GSI8 deadline (`FILE_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`/`PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS` are deliberately
  654:    * the same window, `document-file.ts`'s own doc comment) — a replay years-late must never
  655:    * reopen a fresh 600s window past when the reconciliation worker could already have declared
  656:    * TIMEOUT. */
  657:   private async computeUploadOffer(file: DocumentFile): Promise<Pick<SubmitEvidenceResult, "uploadUrl" | "requiredHeaders">> {
  658:     if (file.scanStatus !== "PENDING_UPLOAD") return {};
  659:     const dueAtIso = file.GSI8SK?.split("#TENANT#")[0];
  660:     if (!dueAtIso) return {}; // Defensive — a PENDING_UPLOAD file always has a GSI8 pointer (deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue), but never assume it over trusting the read.
  661:     const remainingSeconds = (Date.parse(dueAtIso) - Date.parse(this.now())) / 1000 - PRESIGN_SAFETY_MARGIN_SECONDS;
  662:     if (remainingSeconds <= 0) return {};
  663:     const presigned = await this.signer.presignUpload({
  664:       bucket: file.quarantineObject.bucket,
  665:       key: file.quarantineObject.key,
  666:       mediaType: file.mediaType,
  667:       contentLength: file.contentLength,
  668:       checksumSha256: file.checksumSha256,
  669:       metadata: { documentId: file.documentId, versionId: file.versionId, fileId: file.fileId, tenantId: file.tenantId },
  670:       expiresInSeconds: Math.min(PRESIGN_TTL_SECONDS, Math.floor(remainingSeconds)),
  671:     });
  672:     return { uploadUrl: presigned.uploadUrl, requiredHeaders: presigned.requiredHeaders };
  673:   }
  674: 
  675:   /** Get-or-create of the default storage quota row — same shape as
  676:    * `DocumentArchiveService.ensureStorageQuota()`/`apply-file-scan-result.ts`'s free-function
  677:    * version, duplicated here rather than shared because each of those call sites' own `deps`
  678:    * shape differs (a class instance here vs. a free-function `deps` object there) — same
  679:    * precedent `apply-file-scan-result.ts`'s own copy already establishes. */
  680:   private async ensureStorageQuota(tenantId: AuthorizedTenantId): Promise<TenantStorageQuota> {
  681:     const key = storageQuotaKey(tenantId);
  682:     const existing = await this.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
  683:     if (existing) return existing;
  684:     const created = defaultStorageQuota(tenantId, this.now());
  685:     const wrote = await this.store.putIfAbsent(created);
  686:     if (wrote) return created;
  687:     const fresh = await this.store.get<TenantStorageQuota>(key);
  688:     if (!fresh) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError(); // Lost the creation race and re-read vanished — treat as generic guest failure, never a 500 a guest could learn from.
  689:     return fresh;
  690:   }
  691: 
  692:   /**
  693:    * ADR-0013 (D-265) — called by the guest client immediately after S3 confirms 200 on the PUT.
  694:    * Extends the file's GSI8 reconciliation deadline exactly once (`deadlineExtended`), narrowing
  695:    * the race against the reconciliation worker's TIMEOUT sweep down to the network round-trip
  696:    * between the S3 response and this call reaching DynamoDB — never a formal proof of zero race
  697:    * under any adversarial network condition, the same class of guarantee the rest of the scan
  698:    * pipeline already operates under (eventual consistency across S3/GuardDuty/EventBridge/SQS).
  699:    * NEVER touches `scanStatus` or the scan pipeline itself — purely a deadline extension. Resolves
  700:    * `fileId`/`documentId` SOLELY from the idempotency record of the original `submitEvidence()`
  701:    * call (never a raw client-supplied `fileId` — that would not prove possession of the
  702:    * submission).
  703:    */
  704:   async confirmUploadInFlight(
  705:     rawSessionToken: string,
  706:     requestContext: { ip: string; csrfCookieValue: string | undefined; csrfHeaderValue: string | undefined },
  707:     idempotencyKey: string,
  708:   ): Promise<{ extended: boolean }> {
  709:     const resolved = await this.resolveSession(rawSessionToken, requestContext);
  710:     this.assertCsrf(requestContext, resolved.session);
  711: 
  712:     const tenantId = authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(resolved.session);
  713:     const subjectId = resolved.session.subjectId;
  714:     const idempotencyRecordKey = { PK: `TENANT#${tenantId}#SUBJECT#${subjectId}`, SK: `DOCREQUEST#${resolved.request.documentRequestId}#SUBMIT#${idempotencyKey}` };
  715:     const record = await this.store.get<{ resultSnapshot: SubmitEvidenceResult } & EntityKey>(idempotencyRecordKey);
  716:     if (!record) throw new GuestAccessInvalidError(); // No matching submission for this session — never accepts a loose fileId.
  717: 
  718:     const key = documentFileKey(tenantId, record.resultSnapshot.documentId, record.resultSnapshot.seq, record.resultSnapshot.fileId);
  719: 
  720:     for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_OCC_RETRIES; attempt++) {
  721:       const file = await this.store.get<DocumentFile>(key);
  722:       if (!file || !isNonTerminalFileScanStatus(file.scanStatus)) return { extended: false };
  723:       if (file.deadlineExtended === true) return { extended: true };
  724: 
  725:       // Codex review round 2 (D-265 implementation) — real bug found and fixed: a file can still
  726:       // be `PENDING_UPLOAD` (never advanced to `SCANNING`, meaning no physical S3 event has EVER
  727:       // been observed) while its ORIGINAL GSI8 deadline has already passed — e.g. the guest never
  728:       // actually completed the PUT and retried much later. Extending in that case would falsely
  729:       // resurrect a submission that never received a single byte, and the frontend's own
  730:       // `{extended:true}` == success contract would then lie. `SCANNING` is deliberately EXEMPT
  731:       // from this check — reaching it at all is itself proof a physical event already arrived, so
  732:       // the original deadline having lapsed since then is irrelevant (same reasoning ADR-0013 §3
  733:       // gives for accepting SCANNING unconditionally).
  734:       if (file.scanStatus === "PENDING_UPLOAD") {
  735:         const originalDueAtIso = file.GSI8SK?.split("#TENANT#")[0];
  736:         if (!originalDueAtIso || Date.parse(originalDueAtIso) <= Date.parse(this.now())) return { extended: false };
  737:       }
  738: 
  739:       const due = deriveDocumentFileMaintenanceDue({ scanStatus: file.scanStatus, createdAt: this.now() })!;
  740:       try {
  741:         await this.store.transactWrite([
  742:           {
  743:             Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
  744:               tableName: this.tableName,
  745:               key,
  746:               tenantId,
  747:               expectedVersion: file.version,
  748:               set: { deadlineExtended: true, ...documentFileGsi8Keys({ dueAtIso: due.dueAtIso, tenantId, fileId: file.fileId }) },
  749:               now: this.now(),
  750:               extraConditions: [
  751:                 { expression: "attribute_not_exists(#extended) OR #extended <> :true", names: { "#extended": "deadlineExtended" }, values: { ":true": true } },
  752:                 { expression: "#status IN (:pending, :scanning)", names: { "#status": "scanStatus" }, values: { ":pending": "PENDING_UPLOAD", ":scanning": "SCANNING" } },
  753:               ],
  754:             }),
  755:           },
  756:         ]);
  757:         return { extended: true };
  758:       } catch (err) {
  759:         if (!isTransactionCanceled(err)) throw err;
  760:         // Re-read and re-arbitrate next iteration — never decided from a single cancellation
  761:         // (closes the false-negative a fixed-margin-only approach had: a concurrent
  762:         // `applyFileScanResult()` moving PENDING_UPLOAD->SCANNING is a legitimate retry target,
  763:         // not necessarily "already resolved").
  764:       }
  765:     }
  766:     throw new Error(`confirmUploadInFlight exhausted retries for file ${record.resultSnapshot.fileId} under contention.`);
  767:   }
  768: 
  769:   private async markOpened(request: DocumentRequest): Promise<void> {
  770:     try {
  771:       await this.store.transactWrite([
  772:         {
  773:           Update: buildVersionedUpdate({
  774:             tableName: this.tableName,
  775:             key: documentRequestKey(authorizedTenantIdFromPersistedEntity(request), request.subjectId, request.documentRequestId),
  776:             tenantId: request.tenantId,
  777:             expectedVersion: request.version,
  778:             set: { status: "OPENED", lastOpenedAt: this.now() },
  779:           }),
  780:         },
  781:       ]);
  782:     } catch (err) {
  783:       // Best-effort — a concurrent read race (two near-simultaneous opens of the same link)
  784:       // must never block the guest from seeing the request's info.
  785:       if (!isTransactionCanceled(err)) throw err;
  786:     }
  787:   }
  788: }
FILE: src/modules/document-archive/http/document-archive-guest-handlers.ts
    1: /**
    2:  * HTTP handlers for /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}* — D-143 Decision 4.
    3:  * SECOND public (no-JWT) route family in this codebase (after `subject/http/guest-handlers.ts`),
    4:  * same posture: never touches `RequestContextResolver`/`authorize()`, only
    5:  * `GuestDocumentAccessService`'s token-based validation. Every failure returns the SAME generic
    6:  * response (`GuestAccessInvalidError`).
    7:  *
    8:  * Mandatory response hardening (Decision 4): `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` on every response
    9:  * from this handler set — a guest link/session token must never leak into a `Referer` header of
   10:  * a subsequent cross-origin navigation. CSRF is enforced via a double-submit cookie
   11:  * (`__Host-et_docarchive_csrf`, set when a `GuestSession` is minted) — the cookie itself is NOT
   12:  * HttpOnly (the caller must be able to read it to echo it back as a header, same reasoning as
   13:  * the BFF's own `CSRF_COOKIE_ATTRIBUTES`), but the session cookie carrying the raw session token
   14:  * IS HttpOnly.
   15:  */
   16: import { AppError, ValidationError, toAppError } from "../../../shared/errors/app-error.js";
   17: import { defaultSchemaRegistry } from "../../../shared/contracts/schema-validator.js";
   18: import { GuestAccessInvalidError, type GuestDocumentAccessService, type SubmitEvidenceInput } from "../application/guest-document-access-service.js";
   19: 
   20: const GUEST_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = "__Host-et_docarchive_guest_session";
   21: const GUEST_CSRF_COOKIE_NAME = "__Host-et_docarchive_guest_csrf";
   22: const CSRF_HEADER_NAME = "x-csrf-token";
   23: 
   24: interface CookieAttributes {
   25:   httpOnly: boolean;
   26:   maxAgeSeconds: number;
   27: }
   28: 
   29: function buildSetCookieHeader(name: string, value: string, attrs: CookieAttributes): string {
   30:   const parts = [`${name}=${value}`, "Path=/", `Max-Age=${attrs.maxAgeSeconds}`, "SameSite=Strict", "Secure"];
   31:   if (attrs.httpOnly) parts.push("HttpOnly");
   32:   return parts.join("; ");
   33: }
   34: 
   35: function parseCookieHeader(header: string | undefined): Record<string, string> {
   36:   if (!header) return {};
   37:   const out: Record<string, string> = {};
   38:   for (const pair of header.split(";")) {
   39:     const eq = pair.indexOf("=");
   40:     if (eq === -1) continue;
   41:     const name = pair.slice(0, eq).trim();
   42:     const value = pair.slice(eq + 1).trim();
   43:     if (name) out[name] = value;
   44:   }
   45:   return out;
   46: }
   47: 
   48: function validateAgainstSchema(schemaId: string, body: unknown): void {
   49:   const { valid, errors } = defaultSchemaRegistry.validate(schemaId, body);
   50:   if (!valid) throw new ValidationError("Request body failed schema validation.", { errors });
   51: }
   52: 
   53: /** ADR-0013 (D-265, Rodada 2 achado #6) — `validateAgainstSchema`'s own Ajv `errors` detail is
   54:  * more discriminant now that `docarchive-guest-submit-evidence-request.v1.json` has a numeric
   55:  * range/enum/regex (vs. the 3 plain strings it had before) — collapsed to the same generic guest
   56:  * error, never leaked to an anonymous caller. Scoped to THIS route only (the other guest routes'
   57:  * schemas remain less discriminant, named as a pre-existing gap not fixed here, same as D-264). */
   58: function validateGuestSubmitSchema(body: unknown): void {
   59:   try {
   60:     validateAgainstSchema(SUBMIT_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA_ID, body);
   61:   } catch {
   62:     throw new GuestAccessInvalidError();
   63:   }
   64: }
   65: 
   66: const SUBMIT_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA_ID = "https://expiration-tracker/schemas/api/docarchive-guest-submit-evidence-request.v1.json";
   67: 
   68: export interface GuestArchiveHttpRequest<TBody = unknown> {
   69:   pathParameters?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
   70:   headers?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
   71:   sourceIp: string;
   72:   body?: TBody;
   73: }
   74: 
   75: export interface GuestArchiveHttpResponse {
   76:   statusCode: number;
   77:   headers: Record<string, string>;
   78:   cookies?: string[];
   79:   body: Record<string, unknown>;
   80: }

   86: const STATUS_BY_CATEGORY: Record<string, number> = {
   87:   VALIDATION: 400,
   88:   AUTH: 401,
   89:   AUTHORIZATION: 403,
   90:   NOT_FOUND: 404,
   91:   CONFLICT: 409,
   92:   QUOTA_EXCEEDED: 429,
   93:   DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE: 503,
   94:   INTERNAL: 500,
   95: };
   96: 
   97: /** Every response from this handler set carries `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` — Decision 4. */
   98: function baseHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
   99:   return { "content-type": "application/json", "referrer-policy": "no-referrer" };
  100: }
  101: 
  102: function toResponse(appError: AppError): GuestArchiveHttpResponse {
  103:   const status = STATUS_BY_CATEGORY[appError.category] ?? 500;
  104:   return { statusCode: status, headers: baseHeaders(), body: appError.toJSON() };
  105: }
  106: 
  107: async function withErrorMapping(fn: () => Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse>): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
  108:   try {
  109:     return await fn();
  110:   } catch (err) {
  111:     return toResponse(err instanceof AppError ? err : toAppError(err));
  112:   }
  113: }

  115: function requireToken(req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): string {
  116:   const token = req.pathParameters?.["token"];
  117:   if (!token) throw new ValidationError("Missing token path parameter.");
  118:   return token;
  119: }
  120: 
  121: function cookiesOf(req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): Record<string, string> {
  122:   return parseCookieHeader(req.headers?.["cookie"] ?? req.headers?.["Cookie"]);
  123: }

  125: /** GET /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token} — resolves the credential (layer 1)
  126:  * only. Never mints a session (Decision 4). */
  127: export async function handleGetGuestRequest(deps: GuestArchiveHttpDeps, req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
  128:   return withErrorMapping(async () => {
  129:     const token = requireToken(req);
  130:     const resolved = await deps.guestAccess.resolveCredential(token, { ip: req.sourceIp });
  131:     return {
  132:       statusCode: 200,
  133:       headers: baseHeaders(),
  134:       body: {
  135:         request: {
  136:           requirementId: resolved.request.requirementId,
  137:           status: resolved.request.status,
  138:           deadline: resolved.request.deadline,
  139:         },
  140:         // G02 (Block 6, D-2xx) — see ResolvedCredential's own doc comment (guest-document-
  141:         // access-service.ts): best-effort display names, absent when the Subject/Requirement no
  142:         // longer exists.
  143:         subjectDisplayName: resolved.subjectDisplayName,
  144:         requirementName: resolved.requirementName,
  145:       },
  146:     };
  147:   });
  148: }
  149: 
  150: /** POST /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/session — the explicit human
  151:  * interstitial action that mints a GuestSession (layer 2). Sets the HttpOnly session cookie and
  152:  * the readable CSRF cookie. */
  153: export async function handleStartGuestSession(deps: GuestArchiveHttpDeps, req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
  154:   return withErrorMapping(async () => {
  155:     const token = requireToken(req);
  156:     const result = await deps.guestAccess.startGuestSession(token, { ip: req.sourceIp });
  157:     return {
  158:       statusCode: 201,
  159:       headers: baseHeaders(),
  160:       cookies: [
  161:         buildSetCookieHeader(GUEST_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, result.session.token, { httpOnly: true, maxAgeSeconds: 30 * 60 }),
  162:         buildSetCookieHeader(GUEST_CSRF_COOKIE_NAME, result.session.csrfToken, { httpOnly: false, maxAgeSeconds: 30 * 60 }),
  163:       ],
  164:       body: { expiresAt: result.expiresAt, subjectDisplayName: result.subjectDisplayName, requirementName: result.requirementName },
  165:     };
  166:   });
  167: }

  169: /** POST /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/uploads — layer 3, idempotent
  170:  * evidence submission. Reads the session token from the HttpOnly cookie (never from the request
  171:  * body/path — the guest never re-types it), CSRF from the cookie+header double-submit pair. */
  172: /** GET /document-archive/guest/document-requests/{token}/document-types — discovery route,
  173:  * item 6 of D-173's estado-final-consolidado.md (engineering-only slice, see decisions-log for
  174:  * the full contradiction/pendency writeup). Same anti-enumeration posture as every other guest
  175:  * handler: token validation failures collapse into the identical `GuestAccessInvalidError`
  176:  * response as `handleGetGuestRequest`. */
  177: export async function handleListGuestDocumentTypes(deps: GuestArchiveHttpDeps, req: GuestArchiveHttpRequest): Promise<GuestArchiveHttpResponse> {
  178:   return withErrorMapping(async () => {
  179:     const token = requireToken(req);
  180:     const documentTypes = await deps.guestAccess.listActiveDocumentTypesForGuest(token, { ip: req.sourceIp });
  181:     return {
  182:       statusCode: 200,
  183:       headers: baseHeaders(),
  184:       body: { documentTypes },
  185:     };
  186:   });
